# Knowledge Base (seed)

## Program rules (from scope.yml)
- In scope: api.gladia.io (Highest), app.gladia.io (High), official SDKs npm/PyPI (Medium), gladia.io (Low)
- Out of scope: all other *.gladia.io subdomains, third parties, social engineering, physical, DoS, non-Gladia assets, physical access
- Scanner output alone is REJECTED — every finding must be manually validated
- Passive-first: GET/HEAD only, ≤1 rps, no account creation, no data modification
- Secrets in commits: sha256 only, never raw

## Baseline surface (2026-08-07 passive recon)
- gladia.io -> 301 to www.gladia.io (Vercel, HSTS max-age=63072000, x-vercel-id)
- api.gladia.io: 404 JSON on /, CORS access-control-allow-origin: *, exposes x-gladia-request-id, traceparent, tracestate, x-request-id, x-correlation-id; HSTS includeSubDomains preload; no WWW-Authenticate on 404
- api.gladia.io/openapi.json: OpenAPI schema consumed by official SDK code generator (gladiaio/sdk packages/generator)
- app.gladia.io: 302 -> /signin, sets __sid (expired) + return-to base64url JWT-ish cookie (eyJ1cmwiOiIvIn0%3D = {"url":"/"}), HttpOnly Secure SameSite=Lax, x-robots-tag noindex, HSTS preload
- GitHub org gladiaio (19 public repos): sdk (monorepo: sdk-js, sdk-python, generator), gladia-cli (Go), docs (MDX), gladia-samples (Python), gladiaflow (Rust), realtime-multilingual-asr-router (Python), n8n-nodes-gladia, vercel-ai, compare-stt (TS), skills, gladia-quiz-app, normalization, triton-inference-memory-allocation, gunicorn-fix-sigabort, uvicorn-fix-sigabort, 1password-scim, vllm_backend, transformers, cloudnative-pg
- npm: @gladiaio/sdk 1.1.0 (official, from gladiaio/sdk monorepo); `gladia` 0.1.3 claims "Official TypeScript SDK for Gladia" but repository = alexisbouchez/gladia.ts (PERSONAL account — flag, verify ownership)
- PyPI: gladiaio-sdk (Python SDK, sync + async)

# Knowledge Base (seed)

## Program rules (from scope.yml)
- In scope: api.gladia.io (Highest), app.gladia.io (High), official SDKs npm/PyPI (Medium), gladia.io (Low)
- Out of scope: all other *.gladia.io subdomains, third parties, social engineering, physical, DoS, non-Gladia assets, physical access
- Scanner output alone is REJECTED — every finding must be manually validated
- Passive-first: GET/HEAD only, ≤1 rps, no account creation, no data modification
- Secrets in commits: sha256 only, never raw

## Baseline surface (2026-08-07 passive recon)
- gladia.io -> 301 to www.gladia.io (Vercel, HSTS max-age=63072000, x-vercel-id)
- api.gladia.io: 404 JSON on /, CORS access-control-allow-origin: *, exposes x-gladia-request-id, traceparent, tracestate, x-request-id, x-correlation-id; HSTS includeSubDomains preload; no WWW-Authenticate on 404
- api.gladia.io/openapi.json: OpenAPI schema consumed by official SDK code generator (gladiaio/sdk packages/generator)
- app.gladia.io: 302 -> /signin, sets __sid (expired) + return-to base64url JWT-ish cookie (eyJ1cmwiOiIvIn0%3D = {"url":"/"}), HttpOnly Secure SameSite=Lax, x-robots-tag noindex, HSTS preload
- GitHub org gladiaio (19 public repos): sdk (monorepo: sdk-js, sdk-python, generator), gladia-cli (Go), docs (MDX), gladia-samples (Python), gladiaflow (Rust), realtime-multilingual-asr-router (Python), n8n-nodes-gladia, vercel-ai, compare-stt (TS), skills, gladia-quiz-app, normalization, triton-inference-memory-allocation, gunicorn-fix-sigabort, uvicorn-fix-sigabort, 1password-scim, vllm_backend, transformers, cloudnative-pg
- npm: @gladiaio/sdk 1.1.0 (official, from gladiaio/sdk monorepo); `gladia` 0.1.3 claims "Official TypeScript SDK for Gladia" but repository = alexisbouchez/gladia.ts (PERSONAL account — flag, verify ownership)
- PyPI: gladiaio-sdk (Python SDK, sync + async)

# Knowledge Base (seed)

## Program rules (from scope.yml)
- In scope: api.gladia.io (Highest), app.gladia.io (High), official SDKs npm/PyPI (Medium), gladia.io (Low)
- Out of scope: all other *.gladia.io subdomains, third parties, social engineering, physical, DoS, non-Gladia assets, physical access
- Scanner output alone is REJECTED — every finding must be manually validated
- Passive-first: GET/HEAD only, ≤1 rps, no account creation, no data modification
- Secrets in commits: sha256 only, never raw

## Baseline surface (2026-08-07 passive recon)
- gladia.io -> 301 to www.gladia.io (Vercel, HSTS max-age=63072000, x-vercel-id)
- api.gladia.io: 404 JSON on /, CORS access-control-allow-origin: *, exposes x-gladia-request-id, traceparent, tracestate, x-request-id, x-correlation-id; HSTS includeSubDomains preload; no WWW-Authenticate on 404
- api.gladia.io/openapi.json: OpenAPI schema consumed by official SDK code generator (gladiaio/sdk packages/generator)
- app.gladia.io: 302 -> /signin, sets __sid (expired) + return-to base64url JWT-ish cookie (eyJ1cmwiOiIvIn0%3D = {"url":"/"}), HttpOnly Secure SameSite=Lax, x-robots-tag noindex, HSTS preload
- GitHub org gladiaio (19 public repos): sdk (monorepo: sdk-js, sdk-python, generator), gladia-cli (Go), docs (MDX), gladia-samples (Python), gladiaflow (Rust), realtime-multilingual-asr-router (Python), n8n-nodes-gladia, vercel-ai, compare-stt (TS), skills, gladia-quiz-app, normalization, triton-inference-memory-allocation, gunicorn-fix-sigabort, uvicorn-fix-sigabort, 1password-scim, vllm_backend, transformers, cloudnative-pg
- npm: @gladiaio/sdk 1.1.0 (official, from gladiaio/sdk monorepo); `gladia` 0.1.3 claims "Official TypeScript SDK for Gladia" but repository = alexisbouchez/gladia.ts (PERSONAL account — flag, verify ownership)
- PyPI: gladiaio-sdk (Python SDK, sync + async)

import pathlib, hashlib, re
hits = pathlib.Path('reposcan-raw/grep-hits.txt').read_text().splitlines()
# Show all hits with line numbers
for i,l in enumerate(hits[1:60],1):
    print(f'{i:2}: {l}')
print('--- checks for REAL_SECRET patterns ---')
# Check each file for actual secret-like values (long random strings)
# For triage we need to evaluate each hit manually: look for actual values
# Read the files referenced
import os
for line in hits[1:]:
    if not line.strip(): continue
    # parse file:line:content
    parts = line.split(':',2)
    if len(parts)<3: continue
    f, lnum, content = parts
    full = pathlib.Path(f)
    if not full.exists():
        # try with reposcan-raw prefix? hits already include that
        continue
    text = full.read_text(errors='ignore').splitlines()
    idx = int(lnum)-1
    if 0 <= idx < len(text):
        snippet = text[idx].strip()
        # detect potential secrets: entropy + length
        if len(snippet)>20 and re.search(r'[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{20,}', snippet):
            # compute sha256 of snippet for reporting
            h = hashlib.sha256(snippet.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
            print(f'POTENTIAL: {f}:{lnum} sha256:{h} :: {snippet[:120]}')

import os, re, hashlib
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
from pathlib import Path
from github import Github

REPO = os.environ["GITHUB_REPOSITORY"]
TOKEN = os.environ["GITHUB_TOKEN"]
GLOB = os.environ.get("LEAD_GLOB", "leads/lead-*.md")
TARGET = os.environ.get("TARGET_NAME", "hunt")
MODEL_FROM_FILENAME = os.environ.get("MODEL_FROM_FILENAME", "0") == "1"

STOP = re.compile(r'^\[(HYP|PARKED|FINAL|NEXT|LEARN|RISK|NEW|CHANGED|PRIO)\]')
KV = re.compile(r'^([a-z_]+):\s*(.*)$', re.I)

TITLE_SIM_THRESHOLD = float(os.environ.get("TITLE_SIM_THRESHOLD", "0.82"))
COARSE_SIM_THRESHOLD = float(os.environ.get("COARSE_SIM_THRESHOLD", "0.55"))


def split_inline(title):
    """Extract inline attributes from a single-line lead of the form:
    [HYP] Title: confidence 96, class MISCONFIG, verify_steps ..., asset https://...
    Attribute matches are removed; the title is cut at the first attribute."""
    attrs = {}
    pats = [
        (r'confidence\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*(\d{1,3})', 'confidence', None),
        (r'conf\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*(\d{1,3})', 'confidence', None),
        (r'\((\d{2,3})\)\s*[:;]', 'confidence', None),
        (r'class\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*([A-Za-z_]+)', 'class', str.upper),
        (r'asset\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*(https?://[^\s,;]+|/[^\s,;]+)', 'asset', None),
        (r'severity\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*([A-Za-z]+)', 'severity', None),
        (r'testability\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*([A-Za-z_]+)', 'testability', str.upper),
        (r'verify[_\s]steps\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*([^\n|]+)', 'verify_steps', None),
        (r'impact\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*([^\n|]+)', 'impact', None),
        (r'reasoning\s*(?:=|:|\s+)\s*([^\n|]+)', 'reasoning', None),
    ]
    cut = None
    for pat, key, transform in pats:
        m = re.search(pat, title, re.I)
        if m:
            v = m.group(1).strip()
            if transform:
                v = transform(v)
            if key not in attrs:
                attrs[key] = v
            cut = m.start() if cut is None else min(cut, m.start())
    if cut is not None:
        t = title[:cut]
    else:
        t = title
    t = re.sub(r'^\s*[:,\s-]+\s*', '', t)
    t = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', t).strip()
    return t, attrs


def parse_blocks(text, model):
    blocks, lines, i = [], text.splitlines(), 0
    while i < len(lines):
        m = re.match(r'^\[HYP\]\s*(.*)$', lines[i].strip())
        if not m:
            i += 1
            continue
        raw_title = m.group(1).strip()
        b = {"title": raw_title, "model": model}
        j = i + 1
        while j < len(lines):
            l = lines[j].strip()
            if not l or STOP.match(l):
                break
            kv = KV.match(l)
            if kv:
                b[kv.group(1).lower()] = kv.group(2).strip()
            j += 1
        if "<" not in str(raw_title):
            if not any(k in b for k in ('class', 'asset', 'confidence', 'reasoning', 'verify_steps')):
                clean, attrs = split_inline(raw_title)
                b['title'] = clean
                for k, v in attrs.items():
                    b.setdefault(k.lower(), v)
            blocks.append(b)
        i = j
    return blocks


def norm_asset(a):
    a = (a or "").strip().lower().strip("`")
    a = re.sub(r'^((get|post|put|patch|delete|head|options)s?)[/,\s]+', '', a, flags=re.I)
    a = re.sub(r'\s*\(.*?\)', ' ', a)
    a = re.sub(r'[{}[\],].*$', '', a)
    a = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', a).strip()
    return a[:120]


def coarse_target(a):
    """Bare scheme+host+path of an asset (drops credentials, queries, parens)."""
    a = (a or "").strip().lower()
    a = re.sub(r'^`+|`+$', '', a)
    a = re.sub(r'^\[(get|post|put|patch|delete|head|options)s?\][\s,]+', '', a)
    m = re.match(r'^(?:https?://)?([^/\s()]+)(/[^\s()]*)?', a)
    if not m:
        return ""

Gladia JavaScript SDK\n\nA TypeScript/JavaScript SDK for the [Gladia](https://www.gladia.io/) API.\n\n## Requirements\n\nFor non-browser environment, you need either **Node.js 20+** or **Bun** .\n\n## Installation\n\n```bash\nnpm install @gladiaio/sdk\n```\n\nIf you are using Node.js < 22, you also need to install the `ws` package:\n\n```bash\nnpm install ws\n```\n\n## Usage\n\nImport **`GladiaClient`** and create an instance.\n\nProvide an API key with **`apiKey`** or the **`GLADIA_API_KEY`** environment variable. [Get your API key here](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/introduction/getting-started) in under a minute.\n\nYou can also set **`GLADIA_API_URL`**. For live sessions, **`GLADIA_REGION`** (`eu-west` / `us-west`) is passed only on session creation (`POST /v2/live`).\n\n### Node.js / Browser (ESM)\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from '@gladiaio/sdk'\n\nconst gladiaClient = new GladiaClient({\n  apiKey: 'your-api-key',\n})\n```\n\n### Node.js (CommonJS)\n\n```javascript\nconst { GladiaClient } = require('@gladiaio/sdk')\n\nconst gladiaClient = new GladiaClient({\n  apiKey: 'your-api-key',\n})\n```\n\n### Browser (script tag)\n\n```html\n<script src=\"https://unpkg.com/@gladiaio/sdk\"></script>\n\n<script>\n  const gladiaClient = new Gladia.GladiaClient({\n    apiKey: 'your-api-key',\n  })\n</script>\n```\n\n## Pre-recorded transcription\n\n**`transcribe()`** accepts a file path (Node), **`http(s)` URL**, **`File`**, or **`Blob`**. It uploads when needed, then polls until the job completes.\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from '@gladiaio/sdk'\nimport 'dotenv/config'\n\nconst gladiaClient = new GladiaClient()\nconst audioPath = '../data/online-meeting-example.mp4'\n\nconst result = await gladiaClient.preRecorded().transcribe(audioPath, {\n  language_config: { languages: ['en'] },\n})\n\nconsole.log(result.result?.transcription?.full_transcript ?? '')\n```\n\nSee all [supported languages here](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/language/supported-languages) !\n\nPass the **options** argument to enable features from **[Audio intelligence](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/pre-recorded-stt/audio-intelligence)** such as diarization, translation, PII redaction, and much more.\n\n### Async pre-recorded\n\nIf your runtime has no top-level `await`, wrap calls in an **`async`** function:\n\n```typescript\nasync function main() {\n  const gladiaClient = new GladiaClient()\n  const result = await gladiaClient.preRecorded().transcribe('./audio.mp3')\n  console.log(result.result?.transcription?.full_transcript ?? '')\n}\n\nmain().catch(console.error)\n```\n\n## Live transcription\n\n```javascript\nconst liveSession = gladiaClient.liveV2().startSession({\n  model: 'solaria-1',\n  encoding: 'wav/pcm',\n  sample_rate: 16000,\n  bit_depth: 16,\n  channels: 1,\n  language_config: {\n    languages: ['en'],\n  },\n  messages_config: {\n    receive_partial_transcripts: true,\n  },\n})\n\nliveSession.on('message', (message) => {\n  if (message.type === 'transcript') {\n    console.log(`${message.data.is_final ? 'F' : 'P'} | ${message.data.utterance.text.trim()}`)\n  }\n})\n\nliveSession.once('started', () => {\n  console.log(`Session ${liveSession.sessionId} started`)\n})\n\nliveSession.on('error', (err) => {\n  console.error('An error occurred during live session:', err)\n})\n\nliveSession.once('ended', () => {\n  console.log(`Session ${liveSession.sessionId} ended`)\n})\n\nliveSession.sendAudio(/* <audio_chunk> */)\nliveSession.stopRecording()\n```\n\nSee all the [supported languages here](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/language/supported-languages) !\n\nPass the **options** argument to enable features from **[Audio intelligence](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/live-stt/audio-intelligence)** such as diarization, translation, PII redaction, and much more.\n\n### Waiting for the session to finish\n\nTo **`await`** shutdown after **`stopRecording()`**, wait on the **`ended`** event:\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from '@gladiaio/sdk'\n\nasync function runLive() {\n  const gladiaClient = new GladiaClient({ apiKey: 'your-api-key' })\n  const liveSession = gladiaClient.liveV2().startSession({\n    language_config: { languages: ['en'] },\n  })\n\n  const ended = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {\n    liveSession.once('ended', () => resolve())\n  })\n\n  liveSession.stopRecording()\n  await ended\n}\n\nrunLive().catch(console.error)\n```\n\nWhen you need the session id as soon as the backend has created the session:\n\n```typescript\nconst sessionId = await liveSession.getSessionId()\n```\n\n## Documentation\n\n- [Pre-recorded quickstart](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/pre-recorded-stt/quickstart)\n- [Live quickstart](https://docs.gladia.io/chapters/live-stt/quickstart)\n\n## TypeScript SDK for Gladia - State-of-the-art Speech to Text API","maintainers":[{"name":"softwarecitadel","email":"softwarecitadel@gmail.com"}],"readme":"# Gladia TypeScript SDK\n\nUnofficial TypeScript SDK for Gladia - State-of-the-art Speech to Text API\n\n[![npm version](https://img.shields.io/npm/v/gladia.svg)](https://www.npmjs.com/package/gladia)\n[![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/github/license/alexisbouchez/gladia.ts)](https://github.com/alexisbouchez/gladia.ts/blob/main/LICENSE)\n[![GitHub issues](https://img.shields.io/github/issues/alexisbouchez/gladia.ts)](https://github.com/alexisbouchez/gladia.ts/issues)\n[![GitHub stars](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/alexisbouchez/gladia.ts)](https://github.com/alexisbouchez/gladia.ts/stargazers)\n\n## Features\n\n- 🎯 Full TypeScript support with comprehensive type definitions\n- 🎙️ Asynchronous audio and video transcription\n- 🔄 Real-time transcription with WebSocket support\n- 🌍 Multi-language support (100+ languages)\n- 👥 Speaker diarization\n- 🔤 Translation capabilities\n- 📝 Audio summarization\n- 😊 Sentiment analysis\n- 🏷️ Named entity recognition\n- 🛡️ Content moderation\n- 📚 Chapter generation\n\n## Installation\n\n```bash\nnpm install gladia\n# or\nyarn add gladia\n```\n\n## Quick Start\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from 'gladia';\n\nconst client = new GladiaClient({\n  apiKey: process.env.GLADIA_API_KEY || 'your-api-key',\n  language: 'en',\n  diarization: true,\n});\n\n// Transcribe a YouTube video\nconst result = await client.transcribeVideo(\n  'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DYyY8Nh3TQE'\n);\n\n// Print the full transcription\nconsole.log(result.result.transcription.full_transcript);\n\n// Print speaker information\nif (result.result.speakers && result.result.speakers.length > 0) {\n  result.result.speakers.forEach(speaker => {\n    console.log(\n      `Speaker ${speaker.label}: ${speaker.confidence * 100}% confidence`\n    );\n  });\n}\n\n// Print segments with timing\nresult.result.transcription.segments.forEach(segment => {\n  console.log(`[${segment.start}s - ${segment.end}s] ${segment.text}`);\n});\n```\n\n## API Reference\n\n### Client Configuration\n\n```typescript\ninterface GladiaConfig {\n  apiKey: string; // Required: Your Gladia API key\n  baseUrl?: string; // Optional: Custom API base URL (defaults to https://api.gladia.io/v2)\n  language?: string; // Optional: Default language for transcription\n  diarization?: boolean; // Optional: Enable speaker diarization\n  diarizationConfig?: {\n    // Optional: Diarization configuration\n    number_of_speakers?: number;\n    min_speakers?: number;\n    max_speakers?: number;\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### Methods\n\n#### Audio Transcription\n\n```typescript\ntranscribeAudio(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  options?: TranscriptionOptions\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n```\n\n#### Video Transcription\n\n```typescript\ntranscribeVideo(\n  videoUrl: string,\n  options?: TranscriptionOptions\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n```\n\n#### File Upload Transcription\n\n```typescript\ntranscribeFile(\n  file: File | Blob,\n  options?: TranscriptionOptions\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n```\n\n#### Get Transcription Result\n\n```typescript\ngetTranscription(\n  id: string\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n```\n\n#### Delete Transcription\n\n```typescript\ndeleteTranscription(\n  id: string\n): Promise<void>\n```\n\n#### Real-time Transcription\n\n```typescript\ncreateRealTimeTranscription(\n  options?: RealTimeTranscriptionOptions\n): WebSocket & {\n  sendAudio: (audioChunk: ArrayBuffer | Blob) => void;\n  stopRecording: () => void;\n}\n```\n\n#### Audio Intelligence Features\n\n```typescript\ntranslateAudio(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  targetLanguages: string[] | string,\n  options?: Omit<TranscriptionOptions, 'translation'>\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n\nsummarizeAudio(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  options?: Omit<TranscriptionOptions, 'summarization'>\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n\nanalyzeSentiment(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  options?: Omit<TranscriptionOptions, 'sentiment_analysis'>\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n\ndetectEntities(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  options?: Omit<TranscriptionOptions, 'named_entity_recognition'>\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n\nmoderateContent(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  options?: Omit<TranscriptionOptions, 'content_moderation'>\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n\ngenerateChapters(\n  audioUrl: string,\n  options?: Omit<TranscriptionOptions, 'chapterization'>\n): Promise<TranscriptionResult>\n```\n\n### Response Structure\n\nThe SDK follows the official Gladia API v2 response structure:\n\n```typescript\ninterface TranscriptionResult {\n  id: string;\n  status: 'created' | 'processing' | 'done' | 'error';\n\n  result?: {\n    transcription: {\n      full_transcript: string;\n      segments: Segment[];\n    };\n\n    speakers?: Speaker[];\n\n    summary?: {\n      text: string;\n    };\n\n    sentiment?: {\n      score: number;\n      label: string;\n    };\n\n    entities?: Array<{\n      text: string;\n      type: string;\n      start: number;\n      end: number;\n    }>;\n\n    moderation?: {\n      categories: Array<{\n        name: string;\n        confidence: number;\n      }>;\n    };\n\n    chapters?: Array<{\n      title: string;\n      start: number;\n      end: number;\n      summary: string;\n    }>;\n\n    subtitles?: {\n      srt?: string;\n      vtt?: string;\n    };\n\n    translations?: Record<string, string>;\n  };\n\n  audio?: {\n    duration: number;\n    language: string;\n  };\n\n  error?: {\n    message: string;\n    code: string;\n  };\n}\n```\n\n## Examples\n\n### YouTube Video Transcription\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from 'gladia';\n\n// Initialize client\nconst client = new GladiaClient({\n  apiKey: process.env.GLADIA_API_KEY,\n  language: 'en',\n});\n\n// Transcribe a YouTube video\nconst result = await client.transcribeVideo(\n  'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VIDEO_ID'\n);\n\n// Check if transcription is complete\nif (result.status === 'done' && result.result?.transcription) {\n  console.log(result.result.transcription.full_transcript);\n}\n```\n\n### Real-time Transcription\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from 'gladia';\n\n// Initialize client\nconst client = new GladiaClient({\n  apiKey: process.env.GLADIA_API_KEY,\n});\n\n// Create WebSocket for real-time transcription\nconst socket = client.createRealTimeTranscription({\n  language: 'en',\n  toggle_diarization: true,\n  toggle_interim_results: true,\n});\n\n// Handle incoming messages\nsocket.onmessage = event => {\n  const data = JSON.parse(event.data);\n\n  if (data.type === 'transcript' && data.is_final) {\n    console.log(`Transcript: ${data.text}`);\n  }\n};\n\n// Start recording after connection is established\nsocket.onopen = () => {\n  // For example purposes - assuming we have audio data chunks\n  // In a real app, you would get these from a microphone\n  const audioChunk = new Uint8Array([\n    /* audio data */\n  ]);\n  socket.sendAudio(audioChunk.buffer);\n\n  // Later when done recording\n  socket.stopRecording();\n};\n\n// Close connection when done\nsocket.onclose = () => {\n  console.log('Transcription session ended');\n};\n```\n\n### Audio Intelligence\n\n```typescript\nimport { GladiaClient } from 'gladia';\n\n// Initialize client\nconst client = new GladiaClient({\n  apiKey: process.env.GLADIA_API_KEY,\n});\n\n// Translation\nconst translation = await client.translateAudio(\n  'https://example.com/audio.mp3',\n  ['es', 'fr', 'de']\n);\n\n// Summarization\nconst summary = await client.summarizeAudio('https://example.com/audio.mp3');\nconsole.log(summary.result?.summary?.text);\n\n// Sentiment Analysis\nconst sentiment = await client.analyzeSentiment(\n  'https://example.com/audio.mp3'\n);\nconsole.log(sentiment.result?.sentiment?.label);\n\n// Entity Recognition\nconst entities = await client.detectEntities('https://example.com/audio.mp3');\nentities.result?.entities?.forEach(entity => {\n  console.log(`${entity.text} (${entity.type})`);\n});\n\n// Content Moderation\nconst moderation = await client.moderateContent(\n  'https://example.com/audio.mp3'\n);\n\n// Chapter Generation\nconst chapters = await client.generateChapters('https://example.com/audio.mp3');\nchapters.result?.chapters?.forEach(chapter => {\n  console.log(`${chapter.title} (${chapter.start}s - ${chapter.end}s)`);\n  console.log(chapter.summary);\n});\n```\n\n## Error Handling\n\nThe SDK uses a custom `GladiaError` class for error handling:\n\n```typescript\ntry {\n  const result = await client.transcribeAudio('https://example.com/audio.mp3');\n} catch (error) {\n  if (error instanceof GladiaError) {\n    console.error(`Error ${error.statusCode}: ${error.message}`);\n\n    // Handle specific error cases\n    if (error.statusCode === 401) {\n      console.error('Invalid API key');\n    } else if (error.statusCode === 429) {\n      console.error('Rate limit exceeded');\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Contributing\n\nContributions are welcome!
